
## Batch wave1-mx (2026-09-23)


## Batch wave1-mx (2026-09-23)
Universidad Autónoma Chapingo (universidad-autonoma-chapingo): 27 professors. Source: https://fitotecnia.posgrado.chapingo.edu.mx/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/PlanEstudiosDCH.pdf (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Doctoral program academic staff: 25 Nucleo Academico Basico members + 2 colaboradores, all inside the official 'Personal academico' section of the study plan. Names re-ordered from printed surname-first uppercase into given-name-first form; accents retained. Five names verified against a fresh fetch.
Universidad Autónoma de Baja California (universidad-autonoma-de-baja-california): 23 professors. Source: https://www.uabchumanidades.com/directorio-interno (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Faculty-role holders only (academic/program/tutoring/research roles); secretarial, computing-center and support staff excluded. uabchumanidades.com is the official faculty web property (linked from UABC's cgfp.uabc.mx program portal). All 23 names verified against a fresh fetch.
Universidad Autónoma de Campeche (universidad-autonoma-de-campeche): 16 professors. Source: http://fh.uacam.mx/view/download?file=117/adjuntos/Cuerpos_Colegiados.pdf&tipo=paginas (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Professors serving in the collegial bodies (Consejo Universitario, Consejo Tecnico, Academias) of the Faculty of Humanities. Source periods 2015-2019; a targeted 2026-09-23 search found no newer official named roster, so this official document is retained with its date. Titles neutralized to Profesor(a) forms. All 16 names verified against a fresh fetch.
Universidad Autónoma de Chiapas (universidad-autonoma-de-chiapas): 17 professors. Source: http://negocios.unach.mx/wp-content/uploads/2021/08/CONVOCATORIA-CONSEJERO-UNIVERSITARIO-profesor-de-asignatura-1.pdf (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Official 2021 list of profesores de asignatura eligible to serve as consejero universitario, per the Faculty of Business Academic Secretariat. The faculty reports 63 docentes in aggregate but publishes no newer named roster (checked 2026-09-23). Title uses the document's own inclusive wording 'Profesor(a) de Asignatura'. All 17 names verified 1:1 against a fresh fetch.
Universidad Autónoma de Coahuila (universidad-autonoma-de-coahuila): 33 professors. Source: http://www2.uadec.mx/transparencia/sassit/docs/EscuelasFacultadeseInstitutosUT.pdf (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Teaching staff of the Faculty of Political and Social Sciences, Torreon, from the June 2023 transparency staff list. Academic secretary excluded (administrative role). Titles standardized to source-grounded neutral forms: 'Profesor(a) de Ensenanza Superior' for teaching staff, 'Tecnico en Ensenanza' and three explicit coordinator titles preserved. Five names verified against a fresh fetch.
Universidad Autónoma de la Ciudad de México (universidad-autonoma-de-la-ciudad-de-mexico): 21 professors. Source: https://www.uacm.edu.mx/Portals/6/padrones/2021/definitivos/CCyT_(CH)_Padron_profesores.pdf (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Official 2021 professor register (padron) for the College of Science and Technology, Plantel Centro Historico; includes active, leave and sabbatical faculty as listed. Title neutralized to 'Profesor(a) de Tiempo Completo' matching the source category TIEMPO COMPLETO; source-listed academy in dept. All 21 records verified against a fresh fetch.
Universidad Autónoma de Nayarit (universidad-autonoma-de-nayarit): 19 professors. Source: http://www.uan.edu.mx/d/a/sip/posgrados/derecho_maestria/Relacion_de_Profesores_por_LGAC.pdf (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: 19 unique PTC (Profesores de Tiempo Completo) of the Law graduate program, deduplicated across LGAC rows. The document's title confirms all listed are PTC. LGACs are research/work lines, not taught courses, so courses is empty. Title neutralized to 'Profesor(a) de Tiempo Completo'. Five names verified against a fresh fetch.
Universidad Autónoma Metropolitana (universidad-autonoma-metropolitana): 48 professors. Source: http://mat.izt.uam.mx/mat/index.php/profesores-por-tiempo-determinado (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Two scopes: 44 professors from the official 'Profesores por Tiempo Determinado' page of the Department of Mathematics (Iztapalapa); 4 from the official CyAD (Xochimilco) professor catalog. One corrupted source row ('P??rez Lopez Javier') excluded rather than transcribed. All names verified against fresh fetches.
Universidad de Ciencias y Artes de Chiapas (universidad-de-ciencias-y-artes-de-chiapas): 82 professors. Source: https://icbiol.unicach.mx//views/images/source/PadronAcademicos2022.pdf (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: 2022 official academic register of the Institute of Biological Sciences, verified 1:1 against a fresh fetch. Titles reflect the source's own GRADO_MAXIMO_ESTUDIO column (Doctorado/Maestria/Licenciatura/Tecnico); two records have no degree shown, one is labelled TECNICO. Names kept surname-first as printed.
Universidad de Guadalajara (universidad-de-guadalajara): 218 professors. Source: http://www.cucei.udg.mx/es/comunidad/academicos1 (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Scope: the CUCEI academic directory lists 1200+ academics; per the enormous-directory rule this batch covers two large departments: Ciencias Computacionales (123) and Fisica (95). All names verified against a fresh fetch of the official directory.
Universidad de Guanajuato (universidad-de-guanajuato): 42 professors. Source: http://www.dcne.ugto.mx/dq/profesores (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Official department professors page (source last updated 2022-04-06; no newer update on the page). 'Gonzalez Garcia Gerardo' appears twice in the source and is stored once. All 42 unique names verified 1:1 against a fresh 2026-09-23 fetch.
Universidad Juárez Autónoma de Tabasco (universidad-juarez-autonoma-de-tabasco): 11 professors. Source: http://archivos.ujat.mx/2015/div_dacsyh/maestria_filosofia/c_NUCLEO_ACADEMICO_BASICO.pdf (retrieved 2026-09-23). Public university faculty directory. Note: Core academic members of the Philosophy master's program nucleus (2015 official roster; no newer official named roster found). Five invited external participants explicitly marked as externals were removed. Title neutralized to 'Profesor(a)'. Five names verified against a fresh fetch.

Full collection is blocked by the site technical issue. Do not present these 13 as representative of the institution's faculty.
